[McSecDomainResolve4ColumnTest_alterTableAddColumn_1]
context:
{
  "mcSchemaStyle" : true
}
sql:
alter table `test`.`user_table` add columns (`address` varchar(255));
expect:
{
  "contains": [
    {
      "sqlType": "ADD_COLUMN",
      "auditKind": "CREATE",
      "schema": "test",
      "table": "user_table",
      "column": "address",
      "typeName": "varchar",
      "typeDesc": "varchar(255)",
      "nullable": true,
      "primary": false,
      "unique": false,
      "foreign": false,
      "index": false,
      "length": "255",
      "auto": false,
      "zerofill": false,
      "unsigned": false,
      "sqlTarget": "Column"
    }
  ]
}
----------

[McSecDomainResolve4DeleteTest_delete_hasSubQuery_1]
context:
{
  "mcSchemaStyle" : true
}
sql:
delete from test1 where id = (select max(id) from test2)
expect:
{
  "contains": [
    {
      "sqlType": "DELETE",
      "auditKind": "DML",
      "hasWith": false,
      "hasWhere": true,
      "whereColumns": [
        "id"
      ],
      "selectInWhere": true,
      "joinType": "NONE",
      "hasUnion": false,
      "table": "test1",
      "multiDelete": false,
      "sqlTarget": "Delete"
    },
    {
      "sqlType": "SELECT",
      "auditKind": "QUERY",
      "options": {},
      "hasWith": false,
      "hasWhere": false,
      "whereColumns": [],
      "selectInWhere": false,
      "joinType": "NONE",
      "hasUnion": false,
      "simpleSelect": false,
      "mode": "SUB_WHERE",
      "virtual": false,
      "hasAs": false,
      "hasSelectAll": false,
      "selectInSelect": false,
      "funcInSelect": true,
      "exprInSelect": false,
      "selectColumns": [
        "id"
      ],
      "selectVariables": [],
      "selectFunc": [
        "max"
      ],
      "selectValue": [],
      "selectInFrom": false,
      "emptyFrom": false,
      "selectItems": [],
      "joinTypes": [],
      "hasLimit": false,
      "sqlTarget": "Query"
    },
    {
      "sqlType": "CALL_PROG_OBJ",
      "auditKind": "CALL",
      "callName": "max",
      "func": true,
      "args": [
        "id"
      ],
      "emptyArg": false,
      "sqlTarget": "Call"
    }
  ]
}
----------

[McSecDomainResolve4OtherTest_createView2]
context:
{
  "mcSchemaStyle" : true
}
sql:
CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW IF NOT EXISTS sales_mv
LIFECYCLE 365
BUILD DEFERRED
(
    sale_id ,
    product_name  ,
    total_amount  ,
    sale_date 
)
DISABLE REWRITE
COMMENT '销售汇总物化视图，用于加速报表查询'
PARTITIONED BY (sale_date)
CLUSTERED BY (product_name)
SORTED BY (total_amount DESC)
INTO 32 BUCKETS
REFRESH EVERY 2 HOURS
TBLPROPERTIES(
    "compressionstrategy"="high",
    "enable_auto_substitute"="true",
    "enable_auto_refresh"="true",
    "refresh_interval_minutes"="120",
    "only_refresh_max_pt"="true"
)
AS
SELECT
    s.sale_id,
    p.product_name,
    s.amount AS total_amount,
    s.sale_date
FROM
    my_project.sales s
JOIN
    my_project.products p
ON
    s.product_id = p.product_id
WHERE
    s.sale_date >= '20240101';
expect:
{
  "contains": [
    {
      "sqlType": "CREATE_VIEW",
      "auditKind": "CREATE",
      "view": "sales_mv",
      "materialized": true,
      "sqlTarget": "View"
    }
  ]
}
----------

[McSecDomainResolve4OtherTest_createView2]
context:
{
  "mcSchemaStyle" : false
}
sql:
CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW IF NOT EXISTS sales_mv
LIFECYCLE 365
BUILD DEFERRED
(
    sale_id ,
    product_name  ,
    total_amount  ,
    sale_date 
)
DISABLE REWRITE
COMMENT '销售汇总物化视图，用于加速报表查询'
PARTITIONED BY (sale_date)
CLUSTERED BY (product_name)
SORTED BY (total_amount DESC)
INTO 32 BUCKETS
REFRESH EVERY 2 HOURS
TBLPROPERTIES(
    "compressionstrategy"="high",
    "enable_auto_substitute"="true",
    "enable_auto_refresh"="true",
    "refresh_interval_minutes"="120",
    "only_refresh_max_pt"="true"
)
AS
SELECT
    s.sale_id,
    p.product_name,
    s.amount AS total_amount,
    s.sale_date
FROM
    my_project.sales s
JOIN
    my_project.products p
ON
    s.product_id = p.product_id
WHERE
    s.sale_date >= '20240101';
expect:
{
  "contains": [
    {
      "sqlType": "CREATE_VIEW",
      "auditKind": "CREATE",
      "view": "sales_mv",

[McSecDomainResolve4TableTest_createTableComplex_1]
context:
{
  "mcSchemaStyle" : false
}
sql:
CREATE TABLE test_default( 
tinyint_name tinyint NOT NULL default 1,
smallint_name SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
int_name INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
bigint_name BIGINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
binary_name BINARY ,
float_name FLOAT ,
double_name DOUBLE NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.1,
decimal_name DECIMAL(2, 1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.0,
varchar_name VARCHAR(10) ,
char_name CHAR(2) ,
string_name STRING NOT NULL DEFAULT 'N',
boolean_name BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE
) partitioned by (aaa string) range clustered by (int_name) sorted by (double_name) into 100 buckets;
expect:
{
  "contains": [
    {
      "sqlType": "CREATE_TABLE",
